金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 05 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國製造業維持擴張，成本壓力升高：9 月 ISM 製造業調查顯示，美國製造業連續第九個月
位於擴張區間，新訂單與就業分項均見改善，反映接單與用人需求仍有支撐。然而，價格指數
由 8 月的 71.1 升至 9 月的 77.9，能源、金屬及關稅相關成本上升，加上供應商交貨偏慢，企
業面臨更大成本壓力 。 後續須觀察企業能否轉嫁成本 ， 以及通膨黏性對聯準會政策判斷的影響 。
◼ 日本短觀顯示製造業改善、服務業略緩：日本銀行 10 月 1 日公布的 9 月短觀顯示，大型製造

業景氣判斷指數由 6 月的＋22 升至＋24，大型非製造業則由＋37 降至＋35。企業預估 12 月
兩項指數分別回落至＋21 與＋30，顯示現況仍有支撐，後續看法則偏審慎。製造業改善有助
支撐日股獲利預期，但服務業放緩及日銀政策路徑仍須持續觀察。
◼ 台灣製造業活動加速擴張，AI 需求續強：10 月 1 日公布的製造業調查顯示，台灣 9 月製造業
活動擴張速度創 2021 年 8 月以來最快，AI 相關應用需求仍是重要推力。國發會 9 月 29 日公
布的 8 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，與 7 月持平，領先及同時指標皆續
升。科技供應鏈景氣仍有支撐，但成長動能能否擴及其他產業，仍是觀察後續景氣的重點。
市場展望
◼ AI 需求持續擴張，關注財報季表現： AI 基礎建設需求未受利率環境影響，而是持續擴張，帶
動光通訊、CCL、ABF 載板 、記憶體、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供應鏈維持強勁成長，市場表
現由全面上漲轉向結構性分化。隨新一代 AI 伺服器放量、CSP 資本支出維持高檔，具供給優

勢、定價能力及訂單能見度延伸至 2027 年的龍頭廠商仍可望受惠。10 月中旬起陸續公布的
第三季財報，若結果持續表現亮眼，可望為市場再添動能，屆時具 AI 供應鏈題材的台股與日
股亦可望受惠。
◼ 債券佈局聚焦 5~10 年期美國公債及投等債：雖然貨幣政策風險 下降有助於穩定長期通膨預
期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長
天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率環境下，5~10 年期債券較能兼顧票息收益與存續
期間風險管理。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級公司債指數利差約 80-90 個基點，雖然利差
偏窄，但高基準利率環境仍提供具吸引力的絕對收益水準，因此現階段5~10 年期美國公債及
投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

AI 估值疑慮引發波動，惟股市仍有多頭可期
2026 年 7 月
全球主要股市 6 月 （截至24 日） 報酬跌多漲少，與5 月新興亞洲及 AI 半導體題材主導下的多頭
格局形成鮮明對比，主要因為市場同時承受美伊衝突升溫、 Fed 新主席華許上任後鷹派轉向，以及
AI 類股估值偏高引發的修正壓力，風險資產動能熄火。以美元計價6 月表現觀察，MSCI 中國與拉丁
美洲指數分別下跌 5.9%及 4.5%，表現最差。中國主因房市持續疲弱與內需復甦不均，並受其對中東
原油進口的高度依賴拖累 ， 拉丁美洲則受前波累積漲幅較大後的獲利了結與美元期間走強雙重壓抑。

美股因 AI 估值疑慮再起而拉回，拖累MSCI 新興市場與新興亞洲分別下跌 1.3%。惟有 MSCI 印度逆
勢上漲 1.9%，主要受惠 6 月下旬國際油價回落帶動。整體而言，6 月股市回檔較像是高基期後的短
線修正，並未改變今年資金偏好新興亞洲、日本與科技成長市場的方向。
6 月（截至 24 日）全球債市呈現「利率類承壓、信用類抗跌」的結構性分歧。全球主權債券單
月下跌 1.03%，跌幅居首，主因華許於 6 月會議釋出鷹派訊號，18 位官員中有 9 位於點陣圖計入今
年內至少一次升息，使美國 10 年期公債殖利率一度升至 4.5%，存續期間較長之利率類債券遭逢重
估。全球投資級公司債下跌 0.67%，跌幅次之，亦受存續期間拖累。新興當地債小跌 0.12%，反映
因美元指數回升使來自匯率的貢獻由正轉負。信用類債券則表現相對 較佳，新興美元債券單月上漲
0.53%、亞洲高收益債上漲 0.49%領先，主要受惠信用利差續處歷史低檔、企業基本面強健且違約率
仍偏低、到期牆延後且供給以再融資為主。

近期股市雖因 Fed 升息疑慮及 AI 類股估值過高而修正，但美光財報及蘋果商品漲價均印證 AI
產品供需緊俏且企業資本支出依然強勁 。 市場近期以類股輪動方式進行修正 ， 顯示投資人對於整體股
市信心仍在。在投資策略上，短期不宜追逐高估值個股，但可於震盪時分批布局 AI 基礎建設、半導
體供應鏈及台日股市。
債市方面，當前美債提供高於過往均值的高殖利率，將可相當程度抵銷殖利率續揚的風險。然因
投資人對 Fed 後續貨幣政策存有疑慮，或導致殖利率曲線短端受升息預期而有較大波動；長端又有
期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供給增加等風險。因此，建議債券佈局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國
公債及投等債，不僅能提供相對具吸引力的息收，若未來通膨預期放緩，在資本利得亦有表現機會。

美國股市：企業盈餘亮點集中 AI，股市評價尚屬合理，經濟成長趨緩但韌性猶存
美股第一季財報顯示，標普 500 成分股獲利有八成企業超越市場預期，整體每股盈餘同比增長
26.2%，創下 2021 年以來新高，企業盈餘上修動能強勁。隨運算需求從大模型訓練轉向推論，市場
資金快速轉向記憶體、光通訊、ASIC、CPU 與通用型伺服器供應鏈，引領板塊快速輪動。接下來第
二季 AI 產業仍將帶動資訊業獲利高增長，而能源、原物料產業第二季財報仍有不錯表現，但消費、
金融、醫療等，甚至通訊服務產業都有成長放緩跡象。
美國關稅談判正轉向以「301 法案」推進，目前針對「強迫勞動貨品進口禁令」，美國貿易代表

署（USTR） 已建議對全球60 個經濟體加徵 10%與 12.5%的額外關稅 。 預料下半年陸續將有更多301
調查結果公布，這將為川普關稅談判提供法源依據，迫使各國供應鏈加速赴美設廠，為美國製造業注
入投資動能，故對美國整體經濟表現無須看淡。
伴隨企業獲利預期上修 ， 標普500 指數前瞻本益比目前仍在 21.45 倍水準 ， 且通訊服務僅在20.9
倍、資訊科技僅在 24.8 倍，估值水平依舊合理。當前市場質疑重點在於AI 產業吸引大量資金投入資
本支出，故縮減企業發放現金股利與股票回購的能力，然而考量下半年仍有期中選舉行情可期，且
AI 產業變革快速，新題材層出不窮，操作上維持分批布局的建議。
美國經濟在 2026 年表現顯示成長趨緩但韌性猶存，2026 年第一季 GDP 成長率為 2.6%，表現
仍優於歐元區、日本等其他市場。失業率維持在低檔，5 月非農新增就業仍有 17.2 萬人，失業率持
平於 4.3%。5 月 CPI 年增率升至 4.3%， 核心CPI 年增率加速到 2.9%， 能源價格仍為推升通膨主因；

同月份 PPI 與核心 PPI 分別來到 6.5%與 4.9%， 顯示上游成本壓力正持續向下游轉嫁。勞動市場結構
性分歧，金融就業人數下滑，然而資料中心、半導體與電力等基礎建設投資，拉動非住宅營建業與製
造業僱傭需求，支撐整體就業數據，消費端方面，5 月美國零售銷售月增率回升至 0.9%，其中加油
站銷售與無店鋪零售商銷售(電商)表現強勁，前月走弱的汽車及零部件銷售逆勢反彈，顯示在K 型經
濟支撐下，高收入群體與特定消費仍表現穩健。
預料新任主席華許在確立聯準會改革方案前不急於升息，但目前利率仍處歷史高位，對房市、汽
車和消費造成壓抑 。 觀察密西根大學消費者信心指數在第二季來到有紀錄以來最低 ， 且美國新屋開工
下降，房市持續低迷，美國經濟仍需消化高利率與通膨帶來的負面效應。

歐洲股市：歐洲央行態度轉鷹，靜待美伊停火與關稅明朗化
歐洲央行在 6 月會議升息 1 碼，並持續警告中東地緣政治風險可能為通膨前景增添不確定性，因
此將今年通膨預期上調至 3.0%（前值 2.6%） ， 預計要到明年下半年才有機會回到 2% 的政策目標 。
不過，與 2022 年通膨高峰時期不同，目前歐洲就業市場並未出現明顯過熱，薪資壓力也未持續升
溫，因此我們認為本次升息後，歐洲央行更可能轉向觀望，利率期貨則顯示，市場仍預期年底前有再
升息 1 碼的可能性。
歐洲央行本次會議將邊際貸款利率、主要再融資利率及存款機制利率上調 1 碼至 2.65% 、

2.4% 與 2.25% ，會議中所有委員均同意升息。勞動力需求進一步降溫，薪資成長預計將在一年內
放緩，目前沒有看到與工資相關的二次效應，本次會議未討論中性利率和中性區間，如果不升息，通
膨因此失控 ， 重新控制將更加困難 。GDP 成長預估來看 ，2026 年爲 0.8%，2027 年爲 1.2%，2028
年爲 1.5% 。通膨方面，2026 年爲 3%，2027 年爲 2.3%，2028 年爲 2.0% 。核心通膨來看，
2026 年爲 2.5%，2027 年爲 2.5%，2028 年爲 2.2% 。
歐洲經濟成長動能放緩，但製造業維持擴張態勢，雖然歐洲央行將 2026、2027 年經濟成長率預
估值下調，自 0.9%、1.3%調整至 0.8%、1.2%。然 6 月歐元區綜合 PMI 自 48.5 回升至 49.5，顯示
景氣萎縮幅度正逐步收窄，製造業活動仍維持在擴張區間，同月服務業 PMI 雖僅 48.9，但也已釋出
跌勢趨緩訊號。
ECB 升息 1 碼對抗通膨風險，後續升息路徑分歧，受美伊衝突引發的油價影響，歐洲通膨壓力再

度上升，5 月歐元區 CPI 年增率已達 3.2%，為防範通膨失控，歐洲央行在 6 月利率決策會議將存款
利率從 2.0%上調至 2.25%。目前市場對歐洲央行後續的升息路徑看法顯著分歧，鷹派陣營認為 6 月
升息 1 碼後、第三季會再升 1 碼，並維持利率水平至 2027 年底；鴿派觀點認為隨油價回跌，短期內
再升息必要性降低，故後續仍將觀察各國通膨轉嫁能力。
待美伊戰事落幕、美歐關稅底定，歐股可望啟動估值修復，展望後市，若美伊停火協議落實、引
領國際油價回吐先前漲幅，將成為扭轉歐股當前弱勢、並吸引國際資金回流的催化劑。此外，美國改
採以 301 條款課徵關稅，對歐洲出口實質影響仍須觀察，惟預料美歐盟友關係穩固，終將透過談判
達成雙邊協議。目前泛歐 600 本益比以 2026 年獲利僅 15.6 倍。待短期不確定性因素陸續消退，市
場情緒可望改善，有利啟動估值修復行情。

日本市場：成長動能仰賴出口，政府補貼支撐景氣表現，股市屢創新高
GDP 動能放緩，受惠族群集中於AI，日本 2026 年第一季 GDP 年增率僅 0.4%，創下 2024 年走
出經濟衰退以來最疲弱表現，市場已將全年度 GDP 成長預估下調至 0.6%。然而 5 月出口年增來到
17%，創 2022 年 11 月以來最快增速，其中半導體相關出口年增逾六成、反映全球資料中心需求全
面爆發，推升記憶體晶片與電子零組件價格走揚，故受惠族群集中，下半年成長動能仍高度仰賴 AI
產業。
財政補貼壓低通膨 ， 油價回跌降低財政負擔，近期 ，首相高市早苗推行 「責任的積極財政」 政策，

透過實施電費、瓦斯補貼，並將全國汽油價格維持在每公升約170 日圓水平。未來隨補貼措施退場，
預期物價將出現階段性反彈。日本央行已於利率決策會議把本年度核心通膨預估上調至 2.8%。然而
在利率走高背景下，日本政府財政結構惡化，根據2026 財年政府預算規劃，國債利息支出將達總預
算的 10.65%，明顯高於前一財年的 9.13%。所幸美伊戰事有降溫跡象，預期日本政府將持續縮減刺
激方案規模。
日銀升息 1 碼，美日利差未收斂壓抑日圓表現，且日本央行於 6 月會議決定升息 1 碼至 1.0%，
主要考量國內物價上行風險加劇、薪資維持強勁成長動能，且經濟下行風險受控。市場普遍預期下次
升息落在 2026 年第四季。然而高市若為緩解財政壓力，可能干預日銀升息路徑。鑑於美日利差未出
現明顯收斂 ， 即使日本官方進場干預 ， 對日圓匯率的支撐效果恐怕相對有限 ， 日圓匯率預估於155-163
區間震盪。
日經指數近期屢創新高，6 月 22 日史上首度突破 72,000 點、連七個交易日刷新紀錄。上漲動能

來自日圓貶至 161 附近，顯著優化出口企業的盈餘表現，同時「實體 AI」投資計畫引爆半導體與 AI
類股行情 。 就2026 年以來 ， 領漲標的涵蓋東京威力科創 、 村田製作所 、 鎧俠 、 軟銀、 三菱UFJ 金融、
愛德萬測試、松下控股、三井住友金融、瑞穗金融、藤倉等。上漲族群主要為 AI 供應鏈以及受惠於
升息的金融族群，但即使過去一年日股飆升，目前以 2026 年獲利，本益比仍在 24 倍合理位置。
波克夏深化日本投資佈局，外資買盤同步加碼，波克夏對日本資產配置持續增加。自 2020 年對
伊藤忠、丸紅、三菱商事、三井物產、住友商事等五家各以略高於 5%持股起步，至 2026 年 5 月對
各家持股均已突破 10%， 顯示加碼日本仍是其核心策略 。 與此同時 ， 外資對日股買盤持續湧入 ，2026
年迄今(截至 6 月 20 日為止)外資買超日股的累計金額擴大至 10.2 兆日圓 ， 反映國際資本對日股後勢
依舊樂觀。

台灣股市：AI 需求支撐製造業 PMI 高檔擴張，暑假旺季與股市推升服務動能
當前臺灣經濟正處於生產擴張的黃金期。最新公布的總體經濟數據與半導體龍頭營收呈現全面共
振，印證全球 AI 資本支出（CapEx）並非流於故事題材，而是實質轉化為台廠的訂單與獲利。儘管
全球金融市場短期受到地緣政治干擾與美債殖利率波動影響 ， 引發台股在高檔震盪 ， 但主計總處大幅
上修 2026 年 GDP 預測至 9.64%，配合製造業 PMI 續擴張至 60.7%，顯示基本面強勁。我們認
為，台股高檔的回檔屬於健康的獲利了結與籌碼沉澱，長線多頭架構未變。建議採取拉回布局策略，

聚焦 AI 實體基礎設施與邊緣運算（Edge AI）雙主線。
國發會公布之 6 月製造業 PMI 報 60.7， 連續第 6 個月維持在 60.0%以上的擴張速度 ， 六大產業
中，僅食品暨紡織產業與交通工具回報緊縮。細項中，新增訂單與生產分別上揚至 60.4（前 59.6）
與 58.7（前 57.9） ，同時原物料存貨和客戶存貨分別回跌至 61.9（前 65.7）和 49.4（前 50.5），
顯示在 AI 伺服器、半導體與高階製造的強勁拉貨下，庫存水位仍屬可控。
5 月海關出口達 784.8 億美元（年增 51.7%） ，寫下連 31 紅紀錄。其中，資通訊產品出口年
增 75.2%，電子零組件出口創歷史新高。同時，5 月進口額創下 605.7 億美元歷史新高（年增
54.9%） ，核心物資多為半導體資本設備，依進口領先出口 1 至 2 季之歷史慣例，預示下半年出貨
動能將再創高峰。台積電 5 月合併營收達 4,169 億 7,500 萬元（年增 30.1%） 。先進製程與高階
封裝產能持續滿載，且透過定價調整順利維持毛利率在高檔，讓獲利能見度直接延伸至後續數年。

主計總處正式將 2026 全年經濟成長率（GDP）預測大幅上修 1.93 個百分點至 9.64%，創下
16 年來新高紀錄。第一季 GDP 初估更達 14.55%，創 48 年來單季新高。 總經結構分析顯示，
這波榮景是由「出口拉動投資、投資進而促進消費」的良性循環。全球主要雲端服務供應商（CSP）
調高資本支出，其新一代伺服器建置「必須向台灣採購」 。商品出口實質成長預估將帶動全年商品出
口總值達 8,945 億美元，其年增率創 50 年來最高水準，構成台灣 GDP 增長的關鍵引擎。
儘管台股指數在高檔震盪加劇，但在 9.64% 的 GDP 強勁基本面保護下，下檔支撐極度紮實。
隨著 COMPUTEX 後終端裝置陸續開出 ， 消費性電子將迎來升級週期 ， 可望讓下半年經濟加速升溫。
我們持續看多台灣股市。

中國股市：製造業需求回溫，然動能僅集中於高新技術產業與出口端
中國 6 月製造業需求回溫，動能集中新產業與出口端，製造業 PMI 為 50.3（前 50.0） ，較前
月回升，並連續四個月維持在擴張區間。主要動能來自新出口訂單回升至 50.1（前 48.6） ，以及新
訂單升至 51.2（前 49.9） ，皆較前月回升至擴張區間，顯示需求端及出口端表現亦有所回溫。生產
經營活動預期上升至 54.3（前 53.9） ，製造業前景保有一定韌性，細項觀察，6 月生產指數 51.4
（前 51.2） ，持續維持在擴張區間，本月在新訂單回升、庫存指數回落至 47.7（前 49.3）之下，新

訂單減庫存擴大至 3.5（前 0.6） 。 另外受到美伊衝突緩解影響 ， 主要原材料價格大幅回落至 54.2（前
60.5） ，外部壓力緩解。
產業方面，新舊動能延續上月分化格局。高技術製造業 PMI 升至 53.5（前 52.9） ，裝備製造業
升至 52.5（前 52.1） ，上升趨勢持續，相較之下，傳統高耗能產業 47.1（前 47.1） ，建築業商業活
動 49.0（前 48.8） ，仍維持在較弱格局。整體來看，美伊衝突緩解有效帶動中國製造廠商轉趨樂觀，
但多數仍集中在新產業及出口端，後續仍待觀察新產業能否有效帶動內需動能回升。
然而，內需數據尚未見回溫：中國第二季以來的消費、固定投資數據下滑，房地產未見起色。工
業生產年增長 4.5%，表現最為堅挺，主要受到政策性資本開支與科技硬需求的支撐。零售銷售5 月
單月年增率轉負， 即便有五一黃金周的短暫拉動，但節後服務消費迅速墜落顯示出居民消費依然保
守。固定資產投資年比下降 4.1% 。 這是過往作為壓艙石的投資首次跌破 4% 防線。民間固定資

產投資年比更劇烈下滑 7.1%。
整體來說，中國政府大規模刺激的可能性偏低，主要政策料是調降 LPR 或結構性定向降準。其
政策核心在於為中下游民營中小企業減輕債務壓力。現行溫和的企業設備更新與家電 「以舊換新」 政
策邊際效益遞減。為遏制消費滑入通縮螺旋，中央財政下半年將擴大赤字，直接補貼居民端，如發放
消費券、加大育兒養老津貼等等。中國總體經濟正處於重組期，缺乏向上突破的動能，但向下有國家
政策工具與新興硬科技的實質托底 。 房地產全面反轉或大眾消費復甦的機率偏低 。 雖政府政策扶植的
新科技產業仍有機會，但整體看法仍偏向保守。

印度股市：外部供給衝擊與內部基本面的對抗，看法中性
全球供應鏈受到中東戰事的實質干擾 ， 印度的前瞻景氣指標卻給市場帶來了巨大的驚喜 。 最新
公布的 2026 年 5 月 HSBC 印度綜合採購經理人指數由 4 月的 58.2 逆勢回升至 59.3，創下近
6 個月來的最高擴張紀錄。其中，服務業 PMI 由 58.8 顯著彈升至 59.8，主因是國內消費模式在
中東局勢干擾下成功「外轉內」 ，內需服務動能十分強勁；製造業 PMI 5 月最終值亦來到 55.0，高
於 4 月的 54.7。 雖然新出口訂單因中東衝突擴大而減速 ， 但國內基礎建設與製造業的 「預防性囤貨」

需求，成功支撐製造業維持在歷史平均水準之上。
然而，內需並無法完全沖刷外部供給側帶來的窒息感。印度高達 85% 的原油依賴進口，中東
戰事導致國際油價攀升至每桶近 120 美元，對印度財政與物價造成極大考驗。最新數據顯示，5 月
份消費者物價指數（CPI）年增率由 4 月的 3.5% 抬頭至 3.9%。這主要是受到汽車燃油價格凍結
政策結束的影響 。此外 ，比原油更長遠的隱憂來自於氣候 。 氣象機構預警有高機率出現乾旱 。 歷史上，
降雨不足與聖嬰現象會嚴重抑制糧食與作物產量 ， 豆類、 油籽等作物的受災風險顯著高於有灌溉保護
的水稻和小麥。彭博與野村等機構一致預警，這項氣候乾旱威脅將在 10 月農作物收成期引爆新一
輪食品通膨，預估將食品與飲料通膨推向 6.0%，並使下半年整體 CPI 衝破央行的容忍上限。
面對經常帳赤字擴大、國際收支（BOP）惡化與盧比貶值壓力，印度當局並未坐以待斃。首先
在貨幣政策方面，印度央行（RBI）於 6 月 5 日一致決議維持政策回購利率在 5.25% 不變，立場

維持「中性」 。央行認為盲目升息防禦匯率不僅無效，反而可能將能源衝擊轉化為內需重傷，因此決
策者選擇保留政策空間以依賴數據靈活決策。不過，經濟學家多數預期，隨著年底通膨風險逐漸壓過
成長擔憂，央行這項「按兵不動」的政策不會持續太久。 其次在財政工具方面，為保障佔人口半數
的農業人口並穩定農村經濟，政府宣布 2026/27 財年肥料補貼預算大增 20%，並階段性減免汽柴
油，支撐內需經濟。
印度市場目前正呈現明顯的「基本面強韌」與「評價面修正」的矛盾訊號。不可否認，5 月高
企的 PMI 與 1Q GDP 的暴衝證實其內部需求並未崩潰，而政府靈活的黃金關稅政策亦成功控制了
經常帳的惡化 。 然而 ， 中東戰事帶來的原油窒息感與下半年季風降雨不足 ， 是極難迴避的供給側利空 。
這兩項毒素預計將在第四季將通膨推向 6% 以上，進而逼迫央行轉為年底升息，更高的借貸成本屆
時將進一步抑制工業與內需需求，也讓股市有評價修正的壓力。因此，我們建議現階段應採取較為防
守的中長線投資策略，以在估值向下修正的震盪期中，鎖定長期結構性獲利。

拉美市場：巴西選舉風險與通膨預期升溫，後市應保守看待
6 月以來拉美市場主軸已由單純降息利多，轉為政治風險、通膨預期與央行動態三方拉扯。巴西
總統大選民調顯示魯拉領先幅度擴大，疊加巴西通膨預期與 Selic 利率路徑連續上修，使市場開始重
新評估 2026 年政策與財政不確定性，也使整體拉美股市表現持續承壓。
選舉方面，最新公布的 Genial/Quaest 民調指出，魯拉對波索納洛的領先幅度再度擴大，第一輪
由前次 39%對 33%，轉為本次 39%對 29%，魯拉領先差距升至 10 個百分點；第二輪亦由前次魯拉
42%對波索納洛 41%，轉為本次 44%對 38%，領先 6 個百分點。由於魯拉為較不受資本市場所青睞

的候選人，其當選可能性上升，預期將不利於巴西股市表現。
另一方面 ， 通膨預期惡化也使巴西降息交易持續降溫 。截至 6 月 15 日的巴西央行市場調查指出，
2026 年巴西通膨預期由 5.11%上修 19 個基點至 5.30%，持續高於 4.50%通膨目標區間上緣。2027
至 2029 年通膨預期亦全數高於 3.0%目標，顯示市場對通膨回到央行目標區間信心不足。
受此影響，市場對巴西政策利率路徑也持續轉鷹，2026、2027、2028 年底 Selic 利率預期分別
升至 13.75%、12.00%、10.25%，較前次的 13.50%、11.50%、10.00%再度上修。財政面同樣削弱
政策可信度，2026 至 2028 年基本財政餘額預期仍為 GDP 的-0.50%、-0.40%、-0.24%，名目財政
赤字 2026 至 2029 年亦維持超過 GDP 的 7%，顯示巴西財政赤字問題持續惡化。
貨幣政策方面，巴西央行 6 月 17 日全票決議降息 25 個基點，將 Selic 利率降至 14.25%，表面

上延續降息循環 ， 但其聲明與前瞻指引缺乏清晰度 ， 使市場更難判斷央行是否會對通膨預期重新升溫
採取足夠強硬態度。巴西央行通膨預測亦同步上修，2026 年底整體通膨預測由 4.6%升至 5.2%，自
由價格通膨預測在 2026 年第四季由 4.5%升至 5.3%，在通膨壓力持續升高的狀況下，巴西短期內進
一步降息空間已相當有限。
巴西以外拉美央行亦呈現類似政策兩難，智利央行 6 月 16 日宣布維持政策利率 4.5%不變 ， 即使
其 2026 年 GDP 成長預測降至 1%至 1.75%，但仍因油價與地緣風險推升短期通膨而選擇觀望。
展望後市，拉美市場在巴西大選風險升溫、通膨預期持續攀升的背景下，後市股市上行空間料將
受限，尤其全球股市近期受到 AI 題材帶動，拉美市場在缺乏成長敘事下，表現料將難以跟上全球指
數表現，故我們認為目前對拉美市場應保守看待。

科技類股：Token 經濟學與邊緣 AI 外溢效應，帶動科技股進一步走揚
當前全球科技股正處於由 AI 算力基礎設施引導的「結構性重新定價」新起點。首先半導體產值
將正式跨越破兆美元大關，市場對於「AI 泡沫破裂」的擔憂缺乏實質數據支持。超大型雲端服務商
（CSP） 具備強韌的資產負債表與 EBITDA 創造力，大舉發債並非盲目燒錢。「Token 經濟學」 打通
了算力消耗與商業變現的阻礙 ， 這些都是科技股的中長線利多。 雖然美國貨幣政策走向可能引發短線
估值震盪，但基本面依然偏多。
根據 IDC 最新報告，全球半導體市場規模正迎來歷史性的結構性跨越，2026 年全球半導體總營

收預估將達到 1.29 兆美元 ， 較 2025 年增 52.8%。 其中 ， 單是資料中心半導體營收就將達到 4,771
億美元，預計到 2030 年將達 8,432 億美元，獨佔市場半壁江山。2026 年全球記憶體營收暴增至
5,947 億美元，其中 DRAM 營收跳升至 4,186 億美元，年增率高達 177%。主因是 CSP 業者為
了搶奪 HBM 與高階 DDR 產能，願意支付極高溢價，推升整體板塊毛利率。
輝達執行長黃仁勳定調的 「Token 經濟學」 ，將技術敘事轉化為可量化的財務獲利模型，其核心
論點為 「Compute is revenue（運算就是收入） ，AI 工廠生產的實體商品就是 Token。 而產生Token
的數量就可以與資本開支互相匹配，進而由算力推估出資本開支的回報率。在 1GW 功耗限制下，
Blackwell 世代年營收約 300 億美元；進入次世代 Vera Rubin 晶片架構後，因運算密度拉升，相
同功耗下的年營收預估將暴增 5 倍至 1,500 億美元 ， 提供 CSP 業者持續加大資本支出的實質回報

率支持。
此外，隨著集中式雲端算力的建置跨過臨界點，AI 算力紅利正加速向終端裝置外溢。智慧型手機
與 PC 本地執行 AI 推論的需求已迎來全面爆發，Edge AI 正為半導體市場創造出全新的高價值需
求類別。邊緣 AI 擺脫了過往智慧終端僅比較出貨量的殺價競爭，轉向「高附加價值」的規格升級溢
價。長期而言，隨著 AI 代理（Agentic AI）在終端裝置的滲透率攀升，半導體將迎來新一波結構性
定價與穩健的增長動能。
短期來看，由於通膨數據具備黏性，市場正重新評估聯準會 （Fed） 貨幣政策再度升息的可能性。
此流動性緊縮預期直接推升了美債無風險利率 ，可能對高估值的科技權值股造成本益比階段性震盪修
正。然而，科技巨頭極其強韌的實質獲利能力足以消化估值衝擊。2026 年半導體跨越 1.29 兆美元
產值已成剛需，CSP 業者 EBITDA 預估增長 13%，配合黃仁勳 Token 經濟學的變現邏輯，獲利增
長將有效消化高本益比，建議可持續加碼科技類股。

能源類股：荷莫茲海峽運量逐步恢復，油價料將承壓
受到美伊戰爭持續朝收斂方向前進影響，市場重新評估波斯灣出口恢復後的供給回補壓力；同
時，近期中國原油需求與進口動能明顯轉弱，使油市主軸由短線斷供風險，轉向中期供給過剩與需求
破壞，受此影響，6 月油價出現大幅回落，相關能源類股表現也出現承壓。
高盛報告指出，自美伊 6 月 17 日達成臨時協議後，荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣出口流量快速回升，中
東局勢於 6 月 27 日油輪襲擊後再度升溫前，波斯灣石油流量七日均值已達每日 1,800 萬桶，約為戰
前水準近 80%。其中荷莫茲海峽流量在協議後前 10 日增加每日 700 萬桶，6 月 24 日單日估算流量

一度達每日 1,300 萬桶 ， 約為正常水準60%； 若依過去兩週平均每日增加100 萬桶的恢復速度推估，
波斯灣流量最快可在 7 月初回到戰前每日 2,300 萬桶水準。不過，6 月 27 日油輪襲擊後，多艘超大
型油輪傳出掉頭返航 ，6 月 27 日至 6 月 28 日前後經荷莫茲海峽出口量一度降至每日 500 萬桶以下 ，
不到正常水準 20%， 顯示中東石油出口恢復方向雖明確 ， 但短期內仍取決於船東對海峽安全的信心。
另一方面，需求端壓力也有望加速全球石油供需平衡。IEA（國際能源署）6 月月報估計，第二
季全球石油供應缺口為每日 310 萬桶 ， 低於高盛估計的每日 500 萬桶 ， 主因 IEA 對石油需求破壞
的估計更高，IEA 預估第二季需求破壞量為每日 550 萬桶，高於高盛估計的每日 490 萬桶。需求
端疲弱原因主要來自中國需求下滑。彭博分析指出，中國 5 月原油需求急劇下降，原油進口量降至
每日 680 萬桶，較去年同期下滑 40%，主因包含荷莫茲海峽關閉期間造成的採購干擾，以及經濟

放緩帶來的需求破壞。進一步看至 2027 年，IEA 預估在供給年增每日 790 萬桶，以及部分需求疲
弱延續下，全球每日將出現 500 萬桶供給過剩，高於高盛預估的每日 320 萬桶，顯示中期油市風
險已由短缺轉向過剩。
受到整體油市轉往供給過剩影響，高盛將 2026 年第四季布蘭特原油價格預測由每桶 90 美元下
修至 80 美元，2027 年平均價格由 80 美元下修至 75 美元；西德州原油方面，2026 年第四季均價
預估由 85 美元下修至 75 美元，2027 年則由 75 美元下修至 70 美元。摩根大通、巴克萊、摩根士
丹利、瑞銀與滙豐也紛紛下修今明兩年油價展望，預估 2027 年油價均價介於每桶 63 至 85 美元；
其中巴克萊預期第四季可能出現每日 300 萬桶供應過剩，滙豐則預期短期將出現小型供應過剩。
展望後市，能源類股仍需觀察美伊臨時備忘錄落實狀況、荷莫茲海峽運輸恢復及中東增產狀況是
否順暢，然而當前市場更為關注停戰帶來的風險溢價收斂，故中短期內油價表現料將較為疲軟，在此

狀況下 ， 能源類股表現料將難以勝過由 AI 題材帶動的全球指數表現 ，故對後市我們亦維持保守看法。

黃金類股：央行增持黃金趨勢未變，惟聯準會偏鷹不利金價表現
6 月以來黃金類股表現持續落後整體指數表現，主因受到美國聯準會態度偏鷹派影響，市場升息
預期上升，推升美元與美債殖利率走強，連帶提高黃金這類無孳息資產的機會成本，使金價與黃金類
股短線表現持續承壓。
6 月 FOMC 會議雖維持聯邦基準利率在 3.5%至 3.75%不變，但利率點陣圖中位數由 3 月時預估
2026 年底的 3.375%上修至 3.75%，同時 18 位提交預測委員中，有 9 位預期年內至少升息一次，8
位預期維持不變，僅 1 位預期降息 1 碼，導致會後市場升息預期明顯攀升。對黃金而言，升息預期

升溫將推升美元與美債殖利率 ， 並提高持有無息資產的機會成本 ， 尤其金價先前在美伊戰爭導致長天
期公債殖利率攀升的影響下，表現已相對疲弱，近期利率預期轉鷹更容易使短線獲利了結壓力升高。
另一方面，新任聯準會主席 Kevin Warsh 在本次會議中並未屈從政治面的降息壓力，反而重申
2%通膨目標並強調貨幣政策的獨立性，有助於強化市場對聯準會政策紀律的信心。此舉雖有助於提
升美國政策可信度，卻也將對黃金帶來不利影響；由於黃金的部分避險買盤，源自於市場對美國財政
負擔、政策獨立性及美元信用的疑慮，故一旦市場相信聯準會將維持堅定對抗通膨的立場，這類避險
需求便可能逐步減弱，也讓黃金類股失去美國政策性避險這項題材。
然而，儘管短線黃金逆風重重，但是在央行買盤方面，仍未見到全球央行對於增持黃金的看法有
所轉變。世界黃金協會所出版的《2026 年央行黃金儲備調查》顯示，89%受訪央行預期未來 12 個
月全球央行黃金儲備將增加 ， 雖低於2025 年的 95%， 但仍高於2024 年的 81%與 2023 年的 71%，

顯示多數國家央行對於持續增持黃金儲備仍具高度共識。就自身配置而言， 45%受訪央行預期未來
12 個月自身黃金儲備將增加，高於 2025 年的 43%與 2024 年的 29%，並創下 2019 年以來最高比
例，另有 54%預期維持不變，僅 1%預期下降，顯示各國央行黃金配置意願尚未出現明確轉向 態勢。
該項調查也指出，各國央行增持黃金的原因，主要仍集中在儲備多元化與風險分散。90%受訪央
行認為黃金在危機時期具有避險性 ，84%認為黃金具長期保值與通膨避險功能 ，83%認為黃金是有效
的投資組合分散工具，80%認為黃金屬於儲備多元化政策的一部分。由此可見，央行買金並非單純追
逐短線價格，而是與官方儲備安全性、幣別分散及危機應對需求有關。
展望後市，全球央行增持黃金的趨勢仍未改變，然而短線上，在降息利多消失、美元及美債殖利
率偏向上行，且聯準會獨立性疑慮降溫的背景下，金價表現料將受到壓抑，預期後市黃金類股表現將
於區間內震盪整理，目前對黃金類股後市中性看待。

固定收益：市場觀望華許首秀，美債利率料陷整理走勢
6 月中的 FOMC 會議以 12–0 一致決議維持聯邦資金利率目標區間於 3.50%–3.75%， 並重申維
持充足準備金政策。然而，投資人所真正關注的內容在於會後所公布的經濟預測摘要(SEP)。在 6 月
點陣圖中，九位官員預計今年至少會升息 1 碼，其中六位預計至少升息 2 次。另有九位官員預計利
率維持不變或會降息 。2026 年底聯邦基金利率中位數由 3 月的 3.4% 上修到 3.8%，2027 年由 3.1%
上修到 3.6%；同時，2026 年 PCE 通膨預估由 2.7% 大幅上修至 3.6%，核心 PCE 由 2.7% 上修

至 3.3%。這代表 Fed 不只承認通膨反彈，也認為利率路徑需要重新往上定價。彭博對於 Fed 主席
開場談話的 NLP 情緒模型亦反映了鷹派傾向的變化。
華許在此次的會後記者會中雖重申聯準會對物價穩定與最大化就業雙重使命的承諾 ， 但針對已持
續五年多高於 2%目標的通膨問題，他強調委員會將毫不含糊地致力於恢復物價穩定。同時Fed 也將
維持銀行系統的充足準備金政策。他也指出，儘管面臨不確定性，目前經濟活動穩步擴張，生產力和
資本投資強勁，且就業市場保持穩定；本次會議發布了更簡短直接的政策聲明，取消了華許認為不合
時宜的 「前瞻性指引」 ，使得此次會後聲明用字大幅精簡 （由4/29 會議時的 341 字減至 130 字） ，
Warsh 本人也選擇不參與提交經濟預測摘要（SEP）。作為領導層上任後的重要革新，他也宣布在未
來兩週內正式成立五個獨立小組以分別對「溝通」、「資產負債表」、「數據來源」、「生產力／就
業」與「通膨框架」等議題進行改革，旨在打造一個適應時代需求且專注於未來的聯準會。

美國總體經濟環境現處於「經濟具韌性但通膨僵固」的微妙平衡。即便 FOMC 6 月會議立場偏
向鷹派，但仍約有半數委員認為年底前利率將維持現行水準。彭博指出，倘美伊停火協議得以維持，
通膨或於未來數月回落，部分 6 月預期升息的委員 9 月可望轉為按兵不動。元大投顧認為「年底前
維持利率不變仍是大概率事件」 ，因近期震盪部分是因市場「尚未適應 Warsh 新溝通風格」所產生
的過度反應。此外，高盛在 FOMC 會後發表的最新報告也指出即便  Fed「升息的可能性雖增加，
但仍非其基本預期」。
我們認為，當前美債已充分反映通膨上行風險，並提供高於過往均值的高殖利率，將可相當程度
抵銷殖利率進一步走高之風險。然因投資人對 Fed 的後續貨幣政策存有疑慮，或導致殖利率曲線短
端受升息預期變化而有較大波動；長端又有期限溢酬偏高、政府財政惡化及公債供給增加等風險，故
存續期過長亦非明智之舉。因此，建議債券佈局可聚焦 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投等債，不僅能提供
相對具吸引力的息收，若未來通膨預期隨中東緊張情勢放緩，在資本利得方面亦可望有所表現。

We are positive on Largan and expect its strong alignment 
technology to continue supporting its expansion from handset lens 
into FA and FAU for CPO switches. The company’s FA has passed 
customer qualification with strong positive feedback, while 
customers prefer to purchase its integrated product (FA+FAU), 
reaffirming our positive view on Largan’s business expansion. 
Management highlights that its strong alignment and automated 
production provide strong precision, fast production and testing, 
supporting customer penetration. We model FAU revenue 
contribution at 3% in 2027E and above 50% in 2030E, driving 
Largan’s earnings growth, margin expansion, and valuation 
re-rating. Maintain Buy with TP up to NT$11,000 (implies 39.8x 
2028E PE).

3-month revenue preview: Largan delivered weak revenue growth 
in 3Q26 with revenue -11% YoY, which we attribute to a slow 
smartphone market amid high memory costs, fewer model launches 
of its major smartphone brand customer, and foldable phones 
released in 4Q26 (report link). The miss also reflects a slower 
handset lens pixel mix upgrade than we had previously estimated.

### Page 3

We expect Oct revenues to be at a similar level to Sep, and to sequentially decline in Nov 
and Dec, resulting in 4Q26 revenue growth of +7% QoQ.

Earnings revision

Our net income estimates are revised by -13% / -4% / +16% in 2026-28E mainly on 
changes in revenues, GM, and Opex ratio, reflecting: (1) handset lens: we continue to 
expect Largan’s lens pixel mix upgrade, but at a slower pace, reflecting the 3Q26 miss 
and slow smartphone market amid high memory costs, (2) FAU: we estimate revenue 
contribution will begin in 2H27, with 0.6m / 5m in 2027 / 28E, along with product mix 
upgrade toward higher bandwidth FAU that should support ASP and GM. We expect FAU 
revenue contribution of 3% in 2027E and exceed 50% in 2030E, (3) GM: we lower our 
GM estimates to reflect the slower pace of handset lens pixel mix upgrade and the 
company’s higher costs on green energy. We expect GM to sustain at ~50% in 2026-28E, 
supported by continuous handset lens pixel mix upgrade and strong demand of FAU, (4) 
Opex ratio: we continue to expect rising opex, while the opex ratio would improve as 
strong revenue growth driven by FAU and FAU’s higher ASP relative to handset lens 
enhance operating efficiency.

Valuation

capture the company’s long term opportunities in FAU. Our target PE multiple (29.8x) is 
derived from (1) peers’ avg. ratio of trading PE to forward year fundamentals (NI YoY and 
OPM), which is at 0.5x, and (2) Largan’s forward year fundamentals: 2030-31E NI YoY at

19% on avg. and OPM at 41% on avg. We apply the target PE multiple to Largan’s 2030E

broadens its exposure from consumer electronics to AI data center that enjoys strong

### Page 9

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Largan
Valuation: We are Buy rated on Largan and have a 12-month target price of NT$11,000,
which is based on discounted PE. Our target P/E multiple of 29.8x is derived from (1)
peers’ avg. ratio of trading PE to forward year fundamentals (NI YoY and OPM), which is
at 0.5x, and (2) Largan’s forward year fundamentals: 2030-31E NI YoY at 19% on avg.
and OPM at 41% on avg. We apply the target PE multiple to Largan’s 2030E EPS and

discount it back to 2027E via a COE of 10.7%, which is based on beta at 1.1x, risk free at
3.5%, and market risk premium at 6.5%. Our target price of NT$11,000 implies 39.8x
2028E PE, the first full year of FAU contribution, which is within Largan’s historical
trading range of 8.0x to 45.1x, reflecting our positive view on Largan’s expansion from
handset lens to FAU, which broadens its exposure from consumer electronics to AI data
center that enjoys strong market growth, and could support a valuation re-rating. We

note that Taiwan AI supply chain also enjoys re-rating when expanding from consumer
electronics to AI data center. Maintain Buy.
Key risks: (1) slower-than-expected smartphone market growth, (2) fiercer competition
in handset lenses, (3) slower-than-expected smartphone camera lens specification
upgrades or Largan’s handset lens pixel mix upgrade, (4) slower-than-expected FAU
ramp up, and (5) fiercer competition in FAU.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 099: 261009_ml_lotes

Source PDF: all report pdf/261009_ml_lotes.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-09
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Lotes

Lighter 3Q26 sales +4% QoQ; improving 
margins; reiterate Buy, PO to NT$2,245

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 2,245 TWD | Price: 1,915 TWD

Sep sales +5% MoM; lighter 3Q26 sales at +4% QoQ  
Lotes posted Sep sales of NT$3.4bn (+5% MoM, +16% YoY). 3Q26 sales of NT$9.9bn 
(+4% QoQ, +18% YoY) were 11%/3% below BofAe/consensus. We attributed the lighter 
sales mainly to industry-wise key component shortage (e.g. memory, CPU, PCB), which 
likely capped the demand in general server and PC. While sales mix by segment is yet to 
be disclosed, we believe server should remain the biggest segment (BofAe: 55%) in 
3Q26, vs. 54%/40% in 2Q26/3Q25. We now project 3Q26 GM at 45.7%, similar to 
45.5% in 2Q26, vs. 51.2% in 3Q25, mainly due to higher raw material cost. 
Gradually improving margins; new platform from ’27 
We project 4Q26 sales +4% QoQ (+12% YoY) amid likely fewer working days in Oct/Dec 
and market demand push out due to component shortage. Yet, we believe Lotes’ 
quarterly GM should gradually improve with new CPU platform launches in early 2027 
and more new products. We project 2027/28E GM at 49.0%/49.9%, considering 
potential margin dilution from QD (BofAe: 1.4%/3.5%/5.0% of 2026/27/28E sales). 
PO to NT$2,245; reiterate Buy; cost pressure fully aware  
We cut 2026-28E EPS by 9-18% to reflect 3Q26 preliminary sales, more conservative 
margin assumptions under material cost pressure, and higher share count (+1.3%) for 
new CB (total NT$300mn) and fund-raising plan (maximum 1.3mn shares). We cut PO to 
NT$2,245 (20x 2027E EPS) from NT$2,800 (was 22x) with valuation base unchanged but 
a lower P/E multiple. We lower P/E multiple to 20x (2SD above historical average) on 
industry-wise component tightness, which may partially cap CPU server market demand. 
In our view, Lotes’ near-term margin pressure has been fully reflected as its share price 
corrected 15% in 3Q26, vs. TAIEX +4%. We reiterate our Buy rating on Lotes as a clear 
CPU server beneficiary to capture still robust market demand (see Vivek Arya’s 15 Sep 
note), with attractive valuation under 24%/36% sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28.

Key Metrics      
* For full definitions of iQmethod SM measures, see page 7.

Company Sector 
Connectors, Passive Components & Distribution

Company Description 
Lotes was founded in Keelung, Taiwan, in 1986, and went 
publicly listed on Taiwan Stock Exchange in 2007. Lotes is 
the global top-tier PC/server CPU socket supplier. It also 
supplies connector/cable/harness for PC, server, auto and 
industrial. Key end-customers include global PC brands 
(Lenovo, HP, Dell, Apple) and global datacenter hyperscalers 
(FAMG). Besides headquarter in Taiwan, Lotes has 
production sites in China (Guangzhou, Suzhou, and Hunan) 
and Vietnam.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Lotes in view of (1) solid CPU 
server demand outlook, (2) ongoing server/DT CPU platform 
upgrade to support ASP, (3) double-digit sales/earnings 
CAGR in 2026-28 with margin improvement in 2027-28, (4) 
solid balance sheet, and (5) attractive valuation. AI business 
remains the watch point with potential upside.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
Lotes (ZYZTF) 
Our PO of NT$2,245 is based on 20x 2027E EPS. Our valuation multiple of 20x (2SD 
above historical P/E trading average) reflects rising sales mix from better-margin server 
business with ASP increase, double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28, and solid balance 
sheet. Our P/E multiple can also be justified by solid CPU server demand amid 
accelerating agentic AI needs, Lotes' top position as desktop/server CPU socket supplier 
to ride on Intel/AMD's ongoing desktop/server CPU platform upgrade.

Upside risks: (1) further ASP increase thanks to faster adoption/penetration of new 
DT/server platform and new product, (2) faster progress in AI related business, (3) faster 
AI PC launch/adoption, (4) GM upside from lowering/stabilizing raw material prices, and 
(5) more favorable FX (USD/NTD and/or USD/RMB) movement to benefit GM and/or 
non-operating items.

Downside risks: (1) slower ASP increase due to new CPU platform delay, (2) slower 
progress from AI business, (3) weaker PC/server market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (4) GM pressure from higher raw material prices, (5) higher labor/logistics 
cost from supply-chain disruption and uncertain China lockdown policy, (6) unfavorable 
FX movement to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items, and (7) peers' more 
aggressive pricing competition.

OCP kick off –  Optical,

Al infrastructure innovation is shifting toward rack-level co-
optimization, creating opportunities in optical interconnects,
advanced packaging, memory architectures, and power delivery
as agentic Al drives inference demand.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA

Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
CPO: Optical scale-up is getting closer to reality. A 64-XPU CPO-enabled
compute-rack demonstration highlighted the potential for direct optical links

between accelerators. As 200G/lane electrical channels approach physical
limitations, optical scale-up will become more important, particularly for customized
XPUs. However, pluggagle optics should retain scale-out volume leadership given
advantages in serviceability and cost. Packaging yield, fiber alignment, and reliability
remain key commercialization challenges.
Advanced packaging: Moving beyond 2.5D. MediaTek highlighted custom ASIC
designs approaching 10x reticle size, arguing that 3.5D integration could deliver

approximately 10x better die-to-die energy efficiency versus conventional 2.5D
interconnects. Management expects increasing adoption over the next 3-5 years.
MediaTek also emphasized custom HBM, proprietary die-to-die interconnects, and
DTCO optimization. We see structural opportunities for advanced packaging and
custom ASIC design services as system complexity increases (link).
Memory: Capacity matters alongside bandwidth. SK hynix introduced its MARS

disaggregated memory architecture, demonstrating a 3-5x improvement in time-to-
first-token through shared KV-cache access versus recomputation. Meanwhile, HBF
targets approximately half of HBM bandwidth but 14x higher capacity, with samples
expected in early 2028. Inference workloads will increasingly require heterogeneous
memory architectures encompassing HBM, DRAM, and NAND, potentially expanding
memory content beyond traditional GPU-attached HBM (link).

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Optical interconnect: CPO is moving toward rack-scale scale-up, while pluggables
retain a scale-out advantage.
Custom ASICs and packaging: MediaTek sees 3.5D gaining traction as 2.5D
approaches physical limits
Memory architecture: SK hynix targets inference capacity and KV-cache
bottlenecks through MARS arid HBF.
Power and cooling: Racks above 200kW accelerate 800V DC, vertical power

delivery and liquid cooling.
October 13, 2026 04:27 PM GMT

### Page 2

M
Update
2
Power and thermal: The next system-level constraints. Demonstrations of 6kw-
class double-sided cold plates vertical power delivery, and advanced microchannel
cooling highlighted intensifying thermal challenges. The transition toward 800V DC
should reduce conversion losses and improve power density. Meanwhile, AMD's
Helion agentic Al rack architecture reinforces incremental CPU demand alongside
GPU infrastructure. Power delivery, advanced cooling, and system integration

capabilities to become increasingly important differentiators.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O or Over) - The stock's total return is expected to exceed the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry
team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E or Equal) - The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or

industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis over the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR) - Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's
industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U or Under) - The stock's total return is expected to be below the total return of the relevant country MSCI Index or the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry

team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

below.

### Page 1

ab 12 October 2026
Global Research
Genius Electronic Optical
Core resilience with CPO upside optionality
Raise PT to NT$1,250 on potential CPO contribution and intact core business
We reiterate Buy and raise our  price target to NT$1,250 from NT$700, rolling valuation
to 2028E and moving from P/B to a P/E-based SOTP that separately values the core lens
business and the emerging co-packaged optics (CPO) business. We believe Genius is
making progress sampling three precision components inside the fiber array unit (FAU)

targeting FAU integrators such as Coherent and FOCI.  We see potential for the company
to qualify the leading FAU module supplier into NVIDIA's CPO program. At the same
time, its core business is outperforming Largan, growing +1% YoY in Q326 vs. -11%
YoY for Largan as its share rises on periscope lens. Compared with the pressure on
Largan's margins from green electricity prices, we believe Genius should maintain its
margin, as its main production site is in Xiamen, whereas Largan's production in Taiwan

is affected by higher electricity prices.
Core intact: periscope share gains offset muted smartphone demand
September sales of NT$2.67bn (−2% MoM, +1% YoY) took Q326 to NT$7.82bn (+38%
QoQ, +1% YoY), in-line with VA consensus. Genius grew 10% YoY in 9M26 against 2%
at Largan, which we attribute to share gains in periscope lenses for premium iPhones,
offsetting legacy price cuts and lower VR lens shipments. We still keep 7% below VA on
4Q26E revenue (NT$7.34bn, −6% QoQ) reflecting muted iPhone sales and no content

in the iPhone 18 Pro's new variable-aperture lens.
CPO component strategy offers a different route to market from Largan
Unlike Largan's pursuit of complete FAU assembly, Genius is targeting high precision
optical components within the module, including: 1) high-precision prism to collimate
light from the PIC into the fibers, 2) V-groove/key slot to precisely hold the fibers in place,
and 3) MT ferrules (high density plastic connector bundling and aligning fibers). We

believe Genius' customer is one of the key module suppliers on NVIDIA's 2H27 program
targeting over 50% share. Our base case models a complete-FAU market of US$1.85bn
in 2028E and US$4.45bn in 2030E, with Genius base case for US$185m and US$668m
representing 10% and 15% revenue share of the complete-FAU revenue TAM.
Valuation: Maintain Buy with  PT lifted to NT$1,250
We fine-tune our 2026/2027 basic EPS estimates from NT$37.2/NT$41.5 to NT$36.7/NT

$44.0 and introduce 2028E EPS of NT$49.25. We move from P/B to P/E SOTP as the core
business now delivers steadier earnings and CPO warrants separate valuation. Our NT
$1,250 PT comprises NT$745 base business  (15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) and NT$505
for CPO (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in 2028E). Catalysts include qualifications at
leading FAU makers and the board approved 7mn share private private placement with
potential strategic investor (albeit 6% dilution if issued). Our US$1.8bn CPO value

compares to US$7bn implied in our Largan NT$5,000 PT.

### Page 2

Genius Electronic Optical UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Genius' CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
Yes, from H227E into 2028E. We see Genius' CPO strategy as feasible and lower-risk than Largan's.
Largan's goal is to supply the complete FAU; Genius is entering at the component level, supplying V-
grooves, MT ferrules and prisms to FAU makers. This lets Genius sell into multiple FAU designs and

integrators, limiting its downside if the market converges on a single winning architecture.
Management believes multi-sourcing is inevitable once volumes scale and has seen early indications
of potentially substantial demand from US optical players, including Corning and Coherent. Genius
has a partnership with FOCI and is pursuing other FAU integrators including we believe Coherent;
management suggested further updates could come in the near term. Some parts already ship in

small volumes, but we do not expect meaningful revenue until H227 or 2028.
Q: Will Genius benefit from further lens technology upgrades in the coming years?
Yes. We expect premium lens participation and product mix to support core revenue growth of 8.0%,
9.7% and 9.1% in 2026-28E. We believe Genius is gaining share from Largan in Apple's periscope
lens supply chain after initially lagging. Apple (70–80% of Genius' sales) sell-through units grew 9%

YoY YTD through August according to Counterpoint, and Genius' 9M26 revenue grew 10% YoY on
a similar trajectory. Largan grew only 2% in 9M26, with Q326 down 11% YoY. For the iPhone 18
series, Genius participates in every lens spec except the variable-aperture upgrade, where it stepped
back because of pricing pressure from Chinese competitors. Management says Genius has the
technical capability and could enter if future specs or design changes improve the opportunity.

Genius offers a combination of an established earnings base from smartphone lens and potential
exposure to the passive components required for optical interconnects. We remain constructive on
the lens business and see CPO as a potentially material addition from 2028. The component strategy
provides several routes to customers, but the valuation should reflect uncertainty over qualified
content, allocation and margins. Our Buy rating and NT$1,250 PT is based  on NT$745 base business

(15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) plus NT$505 for the CPO earnings (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in
2028E). The CPO option for Genius is US$1.8bn vs. US$7bn implied in our Largan NT$5,000 PT.
Genius share gains are showing in reported sales, with YTD sales +10% YoY vs. +2% YoY for Largan.
The company is also participating in every spec except variable aperture.  For CPO, Genius delivered
samples of its prism, V-groove, and MT ferrule to FOCI at the end of May, with initial feedback broadly

on track and no major issues reported to date. Our checks indicate the company is also positioning to
supply one of the leading FAU module suppliers for NVIDIA's CPO program from H227.
At NT$1,060, the shares trade at 24.1x our 2027E core EPS and 21.5x our 2028E core EPS, factoring
some of the CPO opportunity in the multiple rather than in estimates relative to the 10 year average
17x P/E. Applying a 15x valuation to the smartphone lens business + 40x to the CPO option from our

scenario of NT$9.30 EPS in 2028E yields upside from the last close at NT$1,060 to a NT$1,250 share
price.

### Page 4

Genius position in the CPO value chain
Genius is targeting the CPO market as a component supplier while Largan is advancing
from components into full-FAU assembly. Genius is sampling three components for CPO
with FAU makers, including: 1) high-precision prism to redirect light from the PIC into
the fibers, 2) V-groove/key slot to precisely hold the fibers in place, and 3) MT ferrules
(high density plastic connector bundling and aligning fibers). The companies overlap in

capability, with Largan also having PMLA and fiber arrays but not getting into some
components like MT ferrules and V-Grooves.
Genius's optical design, precision tooling, metrology and volume-manufacturing
experience offer it a good entry point for this market. Commercial differentiation will
depend on reproducible optical performance, dimensional tolerances, thermal stability
and customer yield.  It has announced collaboration with FOCI and  we believe has

potential to also work with one of NVIDIA's leading module suppliers for its H227
program.

micro-lens array
Source: Genius
Annual FAU TAM reaches US$1.85bn in 2028E and US$4.5bn in 2030E in

our base case
We update our FAU (fiber array unit) TAM factoring CPO/NPO penetration into
AI networking switches, NVIDIA multi-rack systems, scale-up on compute and switch
boards, and non-NVIDIA accelerators.  We assume one passive FAU assembly connecting
a fiber array to a photonic engine with one unit per optical engine.
We assume one FAU per optical engine and 32 per switch ASIC in our base case,
matching NVIDIA's 32x3.2T engines on its 102.4T Spectrum-X package. Broadcom's

102.4Tb/s Davisson uses 16 engines at 6.4Tb/s, so our bear case assumes an even mix of
these two designs (24 per ASIC). We note that higher switch bandwidth does not
automatically increase FAU count if engine bandwidth rises at the same rate. Our 2030E
assumption maintains the 32 FAUs per switch based on bandwidth rising to 204.8Tb/s

Our base case for the FAU market sums up 4 opportunities for CPO/NPO attach:
1. Scale-out and scale-across switches
We estimate annual switch ASIC shipments x CPO + NPO penetration x optical engines
per ASIC x FAUs per engine. In 2028E, 25% CPO/NPO penetration or 1.0m 102.4T
switch ASICs at 32 FAUs each produces 8.0m FAUs. In 2030E, 55% of 1.1mn

204.8T switch ASICs at 32 FAUs drives a market size of 19.4mn FAUs.

2. NVIDIA's multi-rack connections for Rubin Ultra and Feynman
NVIDIA at its 2026 GTC San Jose proposed a multi-rack option to network together 8
Oberon NVL72 racks for the Rubin Ultra generation and 8 Kyber NVL144 racks.

Feedback at the time showed  possible transceiver or NPO connections for Rubin Ultra
and CPO connections for Feynman.  Our 2028 base case uses 110,000 racks, 15% multi-
rack adoption and 75% CPO/NPO penetration, giving approximately 12,400 connected
racks.
For 2030E, our rack count falls to 35,000 racks as we assume Kyber would have 4x the
number of GPUs, equivalent to 140k Oberon equivalents. We assume the 35,000 racks
at 55% multi-rack adoption x 80% CPO/NPO attach gives 15,400 connected racks. We

assume about 216 and 576 inter-rack FAUs per rack, respectively, driving a market
opportunity of 2.7mn FAUs in 2028E and 8.9mn in 2030E.

Overall market opportunity: 12.3mn/35.6mn generates a US$1.85bn/US
$4.5bn TAM in 2028/2030E
We sum four pools: scale-out/scale-across switches, NVIDIA multi-rack pods, CPO native
NVIDIA GPUs and non-NVIDIA scale-up.  We add 10%/5% for FAUs lost at downstream
attach and test and for field spares, driving a market size of in our base case of 12.3mn
FAUs in 2028E and 35.6mn in 2030E. At US$150/US$125 per FAU, that is a US$1.85bn

market in 2028E and US$4.5bn in 2030E (bear/bull US$0.5bn/US$3.5bn in 2028E; US
$1.4bn/US$9.0bn in 2030E). The expansion to 2030E comes from wider optical
adoption and greater content in scale-up systems, despite an assumed FAU ASP decline
from US$150 to US$125.

Scenario analysis on Genius CPO opportunity

### Page 7

We assume in base case Genius captures 10% of the TAM in 2028E and 15% by 2030E.
The base case in 2028E is consistent with US$30 of prism, V-groove and ferrule content
in half of all FAUs or US$15 average content spread across the FAU market.  The
2030E bull case (25%) or US$37.50 would require near full content of some of the sub-
components or otherwise ability to integrate more of the solution value by that time.
Our bear case assumes only 1% share of the opportunity in 2028E and 5% of the

opportunity in 2030E, with EPS even by 2030E falling to NT$4 and yielding limited
valuation potential for the shares.

Reiterate Buy and raise PT to NT$1,250 valuing in CPO optionality

We adjust our valuation method from P/B to P/E SOTP to better reflect the company’s
core business and its opportunity within CPO industry while the core smartphone
business is demonstrating more stable earnings to use P/E vs. prior P/B. Our PT at NT
$1,250 is based on NT$745 base business  (15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) plus NT$505
for the CPO earnings (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in 2028E) with catalysts from
upcoming qualifications into leading FAU makers and potential strategic private

placement with board approving 7mn share private placement (albeit 6% dilution if
issued). We note Genius CPO option valued at US$1.8bn vs. US$7bn implied in our
Largan valuation.
Our price target uses 2028 earnings for both businesses, reflecting the modeled start of
meaningful CPO contribution. We apply 15x core EPS of NT$49.25 and 40x CPO EPS of
NT$12.72 for a NT$1,250 price target. The 40x multiple assumes the optical business
can sustain strong growth after its initial ramp; qualification, concentration and margin

risk limit the confidence that can be placed on that earnings stream.
In the upside case, stronger premium lens participation and manufacturing execution lift
2028E core EPS to NT$54.32. Faster optical adoption expands the 2028E FAU market to
US$3.46bn; 15% effective revenue capture and a 31% pretax margin produce
approximately NT$36.88 CPO EPS. Applying 15x to core earnings and 42x to CPO
earnings gives approximately NT$2,365.
In the downside case, weaker lens demand and margin pressure lower core EPS to NT

$44.29. Slower optical adoption leaves a US$0.46bn FAU market, of which Genius
captures 1%, producing only NT$0.29 CPO EPS. Applying 12x and 38x gives
approximately NT$540. The downside is driven by both a weaker core business and a
much smaller optical contribution.

UPSIDE (NT$2,365): Lens upgrades accelerate and Genius gains share in higher-end
specs. 2027E/28E sales grow 11.8%/9.1% YoY (base 9.7%/9.1%), and GM expands to
35.6%/35.5% (base 34.4%/34.3%) on better yields and mix. 2028E core EPS of NT
$54.3 (+10% vs base) at 15x gives NT$815. Faster CPO adoption and stronger AI capex

lift the 2028E FAU TAM to US$3.5bn (19.8mn units at US$175); at 15% capture, CPO
sales reach US$519mn and CPO EPS NT$36.9, worth NT$1,550 at 42x.
BASE (NT$1,250):  Core NT$745 (15x NT$49.25) plus CPO NT$505 (40x NT$12.72), as
above.
DOWNSIDE (NT540): Smartphone demand weakens, legacy price erosion continues
and share gains stall. 2027E sales grow 7.4% YoY with GM of 33.4%, and 2028E core
EPS falls to NT$44.3 (−10% vs base), worth NT$530 at 12x. CPO adoption is slow

(10%/3% CPO/NPO in scale-out, no ASIC attach), leaving a US$0.46bn TAM; at 1%
capture, CPO is worth NT$11.

Company Description
Founded in 1989, Genius Electronic Optical (GSEO) is a leading global manufacturer of
camera lenses, providing full service optical solutions, including mobile lenses, automotive
lenses and AR/VR optics. Its production sites are in Taichung (one plant) and Xiamen (two

plants). The Taichung plant mainly manufactures VR lenses, while the Xiamen plants produce
compact lenses for consumer electronics. Optical lens products accounted for 73% of 2023
total revenue, and others include TOF lenses, flash lenses, VR lenses and LED lighting
products. Its major client is Apple and it supplies lenses for iPhones and iPads.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's

interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of
shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our Genius price target is based on PE-based sum-of-the-parts model. Genius' growth is
highly leveraged to the overall handset industry and could be affected by volatility in handset
demand, as well as whether handset OEMs will continue upgrading their camera design.

Genius' margins could be affected by increasing competition and the rise of new
technologies, ASP pressure from customers and fluctuating material and labour costs.
Downside risks include competitors catching up in technology, yield rate issues for new
products and uncertain iPhone demand.
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business. We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from

smartphone OEM clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a
slower-thanexpected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success
below the market's elevated expectations. We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-
expected CPO qualification process at Largan. In addition, if the company demonstrates
exceptionally efficient and highly accurate automation capabilities, it could support greater
market share gains within the industry than we currently anticipate.

Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 236: AIと金融市場、強まる相互依存 ～IMFの国際金融安定性報告書から読み解く新たなリスク～

Source PDF: all report pdf/AIと金融市場、強まる相互依存 ～IMFの国際金融安定性報告書から読み解く新たなリスク～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Flash Insights

2026 年10 月 15 日

1/5 
AI と金融市場、強まる相互依存 
～IMF の国際金融安定性報告書から読み解く新たなリスク～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  奥田 宏二

1. はじめに 
国際通貨基金（IMF）は10 月 13 日、国際金融安定性報告書（Global Financial Stability 
Report、GFSR）の全体版を公表した。金融市場は底堅さを維持していると評価したうえで、金融
安定上のリスクは高いとした。主なリスクとして①人工知能（AI）関連資産の大幅な値下がり、
②国債利回りの急上昇、③サイバー攻撃の三つを取り上げた。 
GFSRは世界経済の動向を分析する「世界経済見通し」 （WEO）と並ぶIMFの主要な定期報告書だ。
株式・債券市場や銀行、ノンバンクなどを幅広く点検し、金融システムの安定を損なうリスクや
ショックを増幅させる脆弱性を分析する。各国の金融当局や市場関係者にとって、その時々の金
融市場・システムの課題を示すものとして注目度が高い。本稿では、GFSRが今回示した三つのリ
スクを整理する（資料1）。特にAIが金融市場・システムにもたらす影響を詳しくみていく。

資料 1 GFSR（2026 年 10 月版）があげた三つのリスク

（出所）IMF「GFSR（2026 年 10 月）」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成 
リスク 評価の変化 評価の概要
AI関連資産
の大幅な値
下がり
横ばい
引き金：AI関連投資の規模や持続性、収益性が期待を下回る。
評価：AI関連資産の規模が拡大し ているため、影響は大きくな
り得る。中核的なAI関連企業の収益は好調で、株価の割高感は
和らいだ一方、AI関連分野でレバレッジが拡大し ている。
国債利回り
の急上昇
悪化
引き金：インフレ再燃、財政問題でタームプレミア ムが上昇。
評価：国債市場は金融市場の基盤で影響は深刻になり得る。高
水準の政府債務や借り換えリスクは未解決のため、財政・イン
フレのショックをより頻繁に受けやすい。
サイバー攻
撃
悪化
引き金：重要な金融機関や市場インフラがサイバー攻撃で被害
評価：デジタル化の急速な進展で攻撃対象が広がり、影響は深
刻になり得る。最先端のAIモデルは攻撃と防御の双方に利用さ
れる可能性。

### Page 2

2/5 
2. AI関連資産の大幅な値下がりリスク  
一つ目のリスクにあげたのはAI関連資産の大幅な値下がりだ。4 月版からの変化を「横ばい」と
評価した。しかし、これは状況が変わっていないという意味ではない。AI関連の中核企業が高い
利益成長を続け、株価の割高感が薄れた点を評価した一方、巨額の設備投資によるフリーキャッ
シュフローの減少や社債発行の増加、投資家によるレバレッジ拡大を警戒している。 
IMFは、AI関連投資を世界経済や金融市場の重要な下支え要因と評価している。生成AIなどの普
及に伴い、データセンターや半導体投資は活発で、関連企業の業績は好調だ。AI関連企業の株価
が急上昇した一方、予想利益も大きく伸びている。GFSRは、一部の株式市場で予想利益の伸びが
株価上昇を上回り、予想株価収益率（PER）が低下した点を評価している。 
一方、リスクが高まっている点として注目するのは、大規模なクラウドサービスやデータセン
ターを運営する企業（ハイパースケーラー）のフリーキャッシュフロー（事業で稼いだ現金から
設備投資などを差し引いた額）の減少だ。設備投資の急増により、資金調達のため社債発行が拡
大している。IMFによると、世界の投資適格社債発行額に占めるハイパースケーラーの割合は、
2024 年の1％未満から2026 年には約10％に上昇した（資料2）。社債の信用スプレッドも、4 月版
GFSRの公表以降、投資適格社債市場全体と比べて約30 ベーシスポイント拡大したという（注 1）。

資料 2 世界の投資適格社債発行額に占めるハイパースケーラーの割合

（出所）IMF「GFSR（2026 年 10 月）」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

現在のAI関連企業の資金調達の特徴として、資金と売上が企業間で相互に結びつく循環的な取
引があげられる。具体的には、大手企業が財務基盤の相対的に弱いAI企業に出資し、出資先がそ

### Page 3

3/5 
の資金で大手企業のサービスや半導体を購入する仕組みだ。GFSRによれば、2023 年以降に公表さ
れた 14 件の取引では、株式投資が約3,000 億ドル、関連する債務が約6,500 億ドルにのぼり、将
来の売上に関する契約は約1 兆 4,000 億ドルに達する。こうした取引はうまくいけば、出資先の
企業価値拡大による投資リターンの増加と売上増加が期待できる。一方、投資先の経営が悪化す
れば、投資損失と将来の売上減少という逆回転が生じる。IMFは、こうした取引による投資資金や
将来の売上が少数の企業に集中しており、特定の投資先の経営悪化がAI関連企業の間で損失を広
げるリスクを指摘している。 
AI関連企業に投資する側の懸念としてGFSRが取り上げたのは、株価上昇が続くなか、借り入れ
などを利用した取引が拡大し、投資家が過大なリスクを抱えている可能性だ。ヘッジファンドの
株式ポジションと信用取引融資残高の合計は、2024 年末の先進国GDP比約10％から2026 年には約
15％に増加した。また、レバレッジ型上場投資信託（ETF）に占める個別株を対象としたETFの割
合は、16％から28％に上昇した。GFSRの補足分析によると、株式オプションの価格から推計した
レバレッジ取引の資金調達コストも、2026 年初めからほぼ倍増している（注 2）。レバレッジをか
けた取引は株価が下落した際の損失が大きくなり、追加証拠金を拠出するために他の資産を売ら
ざるを得なくなるといった形で、他の金融商品にも波及する場合がある。

3. 国債利回りの急上昇リスク  
AIと同様に金融市場で注目が高まっている国債について、GFSRは利回りの急上昇リスクを4 月
版から「悪化」と評価した。特に注目したいのは、長期国債を保有するリスクに応じて投資家が
求める上乗せ金利（タームプレミアム）の上昇だ。IMFによると、10 年国債利回りの上昇には将来
の政策金利の予想とタームプレミアムの双方が寄与しており、特に先進国のタームプレミアムは
歴史的な変動範囲の上限に近い水準にある。 
タームプレミアムを押し上げているのは、財政赤字の継続や高水準の政府債務に対する懸念、
今後の国債発行増加の見通しだ。ユーロ圏、英国、米国では、2026 年1～3 月期に投資適格社債の
発行が国債を含む債券発行総額の4 割を占めた（デュレーション加重） 。さらにGFSRは、AI関連企
業による社債発行が急増し、国債と投資家の資金を奪い合うことで、国債市場への追加的な圧力
になっているとの指摘も紹介している。 
IMFが警戒するのは、インフレ再燃などをきっかけにタームプレミアムがさらに上昇する事態
だ。主要国では、政府債務が歴史的に高い水準にあるという問題が未解決のままで、国債の借り
換えリスクも高い。特に、国債の償還年限を短期化した国では、金利が上昇すると高い金利で借
り換える必要が生じ、財政負担が増える。長期債から短期債への置き換えが大規模に進めば、こ
うしたリスクは一段と高まる可能性がある。 
政府債務が増えて国債発行が膨らむと、国内の銀行や保険会社などだけでは増発分を消化しき
れず、外国人投資家や投資ファンドの存在感が高まりやすい。こうした投資家は価格変動に敏感
で、インフレ懸念が強まった際に長期国債の売却が集中する可能性がある。GFSRでは、これをタ
ームプレミアムの上昇を増幅する要因としてあげている。

### Page 4

4/5 
GFSRは、日本国債市場の流動性悪化を指摘している。日本銀行によるイールドカーブ・コント
ロールの終了後、金融政策の正常化が進むなかで利回りの変動が大きくなったことに加え、特に
長期国債で売値と買値の差が拡大している点をあげた。ユーロ圏と英国では流動性を示す指標が
改善し、米国でも足元は良好な状況にあるのとは対照的だ。 
IMFはタームプレミアムが急上昇した場合、AI関連株を含む幅広い資産の価格が下落するほか、
金融取引に利用される国債の担保価値も低下し、金融市場全体に混乱が広がる可能性を警戒して
いる。IMFは世界各国の銀行を対象に、国債利回りの急上昇やAIの生産性向上効果が期待を下回る
ことなどを想定したストレステストを実施した。この結果、世界全体の銀行システムの自己資本
比率は規制上の最低水準を上回るものの、ストレス後の普通株式等Tier1 比率が 7％を下回る銀行
の資産は、世界の銀行資産全体の22％を占める結果になった（注3）。

4. サイバー攻撃のリスク 
GFSRは、三つ目のリスクとしてあげたサイバーリスクについて、「悪化」したと評価した。GFSR
では金融機関が外部の情報技術サービスへの依存を強めている点を問題視した。クラウドサービ
スやソフトウエアを提供する企業の一部に利用が集中し、多くの金融機関が共通の技術基盤を利
用するようになっている。こうした企業が攻撃を受けてサービスを停止すれば、金融機関への影
響が広範に及ぶ可能性がある。さらに、外部委託先のつながりが複雑になり、リスクの全体像を
把握しにくくなっている点も指摘した。 
サイバー攻撃は高度化が進み、日本でも企業の情報流出が相次ぐ。GFSRは、最先端のAIモデル
がネットワーク攻撃の模擬実験で高い能力を示していると指摘した。GFSRによると、悪用が確認
されたソフトウエアなどの脆弱性のうち、公表日以前または公表当日に悪用された割合（ゼロデ
イ比率）は、2026 年の年初来で70％を超え、前年から約20 ポイント上昇した。脆弱性が公表さ
れてから悪用されるまでの時間も短くなっており、ソフトウエアの提供企業が修正プログラムを
用意する余裕は小さくなっている。

5. おわりに 
今回のGFSRからは、AIの急成長に金融市場や金融システムがどこまで対応できるのかという問
題意識が垣間見える。AI関連企業の利益成長が世界的な株高をけん引する一方、巨額の設備投資
に必要な資金を調達するため、社債市場でもその存在感を高め始めた。技術革新が進むほど投資
資金への需要も膨らみ、あらゆる市場から資金を吸い上げる構図だ。 
成長分野に資金が集中し、市場が過熱することは過去にも繰り返されてきた。今回のAIブーム
で特徴的なのは、巨額の資金需要が国債市場の需給にまで影響を及ぼす可能性があることだ。AI
の成長が金融市場を動かす一方、金利の上昇が今度はAI投資の採算を左右する。相互依存の強ま
りが今回のGFSRからも確認できたといえる。 
以 上

### Page 5

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
5/5
【注釈】
1) IMF「Global Financial Stability Report」（2026 年 10 月版） 、第1 章補足分析1.1「Rising
Leverage in the AI Ecosystem」3 頁。
2) 上記補足分析1.1、5～6 頁、図 1.1.4。資金調達コストは、S&P500 の株式オプション価格から
推計した担保付翌日物調達金利に対する30 日間の上乗せ金利。
3) IMF「Global Financial Stability Report」（2026 年 10 月版） 、第1 章 7～9 頁、図1.7 および
補足分析1.2「2026 IMF Global Banking Stress Test」。ストレステストの対象は29 カ国の

665 銀行。

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), với 
động lực chủ yếu đến từ (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn 
Nhất và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ VND 
(+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa tại các thị trường AST khai thác 
tăng bình quân 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. Lưu lượng và tỷ trọng khách quốc tế 
gia tăng được kỳ vọng thúc đẩy số giao dịch và doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

Chúng tôi dự phóng số điểm kinh doanh AST tăng từ 145 điểm cuối năm 2026 lên 
182 điểm năm 2030. Sau đóng góp từ 19 điểm tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất và 6 điểm mới tại 
T2 Nội Bài từ 2025 - 2026, các dự án Long Thành, Phú Quốc và Gia Bình tiếp tục mở 
ra dư địa mở rộng mạng lưới trong trung và dài hạn cho doanh nghiệp.

Việc mở rộng các hoạt động mang lại lợi nhuận cao, bao gồm hàng miễn thuế, 
phòng chờ thương gia và tối ưu hoạt động tại các điểm kinh doanh được kỳ vọng 
tác động tích cực lên biên lợi nhuận của AST. Chúng tôi dự phóng biên lợi nhuận 
gộp đạt 64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027, hỗ trợ LNST công ty mẹ tăng lần lượt 
48%/17% yoy.

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với AST, giá mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 21/09/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco là một 
trong những doanh nghiệp có quy mô mạng 
lưới điểm kinh doanh lớn trong lĩnh vực dịch 
vụ phi hàng không tại Việt Nam, với 141 
điểm kinh doanh tại 6 sân bay quốc tế tính 
đến cuối tháng 6/2026. Hoạt động cốt lõi của 
công ty bao gồm bán lẻ bách hóa lưu niệm 
(BHLN), hàng miễn thuế, dịch vụ ăn uống và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Ngoài ra, AST còn 
tham gia lĩnh vực suất ăn hàng không thông 
qua khoản đầu tư tại công ty liên kết VinaCS. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030.

điểm và tăng lên 182 điểm vào 2030.

KQKD tăng trưởng tích cực trong 
2Q2026

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), 
với động lực: (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ 
VND (+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Biểu đồ 5. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh của AST

Nguồn:AST

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng tăng trưởng lưu lượng hành khách, đặc biệt là sự gia tăng tỷ 
trọng khách quốc tế, sẽ thúc đẩy doanh thu và hiệu quả kinh doanh của AST 
trong giai đoạn 2026–2030. Theo Cục Hàng không Việt Nam, lượng khách thông 
qua các cảng hàng không trong 7T2026 đạt hơn 76 triệu lượt (+7% yoy), trong 
đó khách quốc tế đạt gần 30 triệu lượt (+11% yoy), trong khi khách nội địa ghi 
nhận tăng trưởng 4% yoy. Xu hướng này được hỗ trợ bởi nhu cầu du lịch quốc 
tế, chính sách thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng bay, trong khi tăng 
trưởng kinh tế và thu nhập tiếp tục tạo nền tảng cho nhu cầu đi lại trong nước. 
Trên cơ sở đó, chúng tôi dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế và nội địa tại các thị 
trường AST khai thác tăng bình quân lần lượt 11% và 5%/năm đến 2030, qua đó 
nâng dần tỷ trọng khách quốc tế trong tổng lưu lượng, thúc đẩy doanh 
thu/điểm kinh doanh nhờ: (1) lưu lượng hành khách qua cảng hàng không gia 
tăng và (2) tỷ trọng khách quốc tế cao hơn hỗ trợ doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

 Biểu đồ 7. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh AST (tỷ VND)

Nguồn: AST  Nguồn: AST

Mở rộng mạng lưới tại các nhà ga 
hiện hữu và sân bay mới, gia tăng 
quy mô kinh doanh đến 2030 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng chu kỳ mở rộng hạ tầng hàng không giai đoạn 2026–2030 sẽ 
tạo dư địa gia tăng quy mô kinh doanh cho AST, với số điểm kinh doanh dự 
phóng đạt 145 điểm vào cuối 2026 và tăng lên 182 điểm vào năm 2030. Cụ thể: 
- Trong ngắn hạn, tăng trưởng năm 2026 được hỗ trợ bởi việc ghi nhận 
đủ năm doanh thu từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
trong năm 2025, cùng 6 điểm mới tại T2 Nội Bài trong năm 2026, trong 
đó có 2 cửa hàng miễn thuế và 2 cửa hàng Memories of Vietnam khai 
trương trong 2Q2026. 
- Trong trung và dài hạn, các dự án Cảng HKQT Long Thành, Cảng HKQT 
Phú Quốc nâng cấp mở rộng và Cảng HKQT Gia Bình sẽ góp phần thúc 
đẩy ngành hàng không Việt Nam nói chung, bổ sung mặt bằng thương 
mại, mở ra cơ hội phát triển các mảng kinh doanh cốt lõi cho AST nói 
riêng bao gồm các mảng bán lẻ, miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Với kinh nghiệm khai thác 141 điểm kinh doanh 
tại 6 sân bay quốc tế và lịch sử trúng thầu tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất, T2 Nội 
Bài trong thời gian qua, chúng tôi kỳ vọng AST có khả năng cạnh tranh 
cao trong việc đấu thầu quyền khai thác mặt bằng tại các sân bay mới, 
đặc biệt là Gia Bình, qua đó duy trì vị thế và thị phần kinh doanh phi 
hàng không tại khu vực phía Bắc khi một phần lưu lượng hành khách 
được phân bổ lại từ Nội Bài.

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ mở rộng biên lợi nhuận  
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng biên lợi nhuận của AST tiếp tục cải thiện trong 2026 - 2027, 
nhờ định hướng mở rộng các mảng có biên lợi nhuận tốt như hàng miễn thuế 
và phòng chờ thương gia, kết hợp tối ưu hiệu quả vận hành tại các điểm kinh 
doanh. Cụ thể: (1) AST ưu tiên gia tăng số điểm kinh doanh hàng miễn thuế 
nhằm đón đầu tăng trưởng nhanh và ổn định của lượng khách quốc tế, qua đó 
nâng đóng góp lợi nhuận từ hoạt động mua sắm tại sân bay, (2) mở rộng mạng 
lưới phòng chờ, đồng thời gia tăng lượng khách sử dụng trên nền năng lực phục 
vụ hiện hữu và (3) tối ưu hóa hiệu quả hoạt động thông qua cải thiện bố trí mặt 
bằng, danh mục sản phẩm và kiểm soát chi phí tại các điểm kinh doanh, qua 
đó tăng doanh thu và lợi nhuận thu được trên mỗi khách hàng.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá 
mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu AST, với giá mục tiêu 76,500 
VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 
21/09/2026, dựa trên triển vọng tăng trưởng từ lượng khách quốc tế, mở rộng 
mạng lưới điểm kinh doanh và khả năng cải thiện biên lợi nhuận của doanh 
nghiệp. Chúng tôi sử dụng phương pháp định giá từng phần (SOTP), bao gồm 
DCF cho hoạt động kinh doanh cốt lõi của AST và P/E mục tiêu 11x đối với phần 
lợi nhuận thuộc sở hữu AST tại VinaCS. Các giả định chính gồm: (1) lượng khách 
quốc tế/nội địa tăng trung bình 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030, (2) số điểm kinh 
doanh tăng từ 145 điểm cuối 2026 lên 182 điểm năm 2030, với giả định AST 
trúng thầu mặt bằng tại cả Long Thành và Gia Bình và (3) biên lợi nhuận gộp 
2026/2027 đạt 64.7/65.5%. Đối với VinaCS, chúng tôi kỳ vọng nhà máy suất ăn 
Long Thành sẽ hỗ trợ tăng trưởng lợi nhuận trong trung và dài hạn khi dự kiến 
có thể bắt đầu đóng góp lợi nhuận cho AST từ 2028.

Rủi ro đối với định giá bao gồm: (1) AST không giành được quyền khai thác tại 
Long Thành và Gia Bình như kỳ vọng, (2) lưu lượng khách dịch chuyển giữa các 
sân bay mới và hiện hữu khiến doanh thu tại một số điểm kinh doanh bị sụt 
giảm, (3) cạnh tranh gia tăng từ các doanh nghiệp kinh doanh dịch vụ phi hàng 
không khác và (4) căng thẳng địa chính trị kéo dài, giá dầu tăng gây áp lực lên 
hoạt động hàng không và chi tiêu của hành khách.

## Document 323: First Impressions_ NZ household incomes, assets and savings update - June quarter 2026 (2026-10-20)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ household incomes, assets and savings update - June quarter 2026 (2026-10-20).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#consumer #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ household incomes, assets and savings
update - June quarter 2026
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
10:24 October 08 2026 Share
New Zealand incomes and savings are rising, as is the value of households’ financial
assets. However, the value of housing assets remains flat.
Key takeouts from Stats NZ’s latest update on household incomes, assets and savings (June quarter
2026)

### Page 2

Household incomes
Disposable income levels in the economy grew by 5.2% in the year to June, down from 6.5% in the
year to March

Savings and wealth
Household savings have continued to rise, climbing by $2.5b over the past quarter.

Overall household wealth levels were up 1.9% over the past year. Under the surface, the make-up of
household wealth continues to shift.

Interest costs 
Overall household income levels are continuing to rise. Much of that is due to increases in
entrepreneurial earnings (which is in part due to increased earnings in the agricultural sector). Wage
and salary growth has been more limited but has picked up.
Household savings levels have been rising for three years now, with another solid rise over the past
three months. 
Overall household wealth levels have risen modestly. Although the value of households’ financial
assets is continuing to rise, housing assets have essentially shown no growth since late 2023.
Household spending on interest costs rose just slightly in the June quarter. That’s the first increase in
two years. The easing in average borrowing costs seen over the past few years has come to a close
with the RBNZ’s hiking cycle now in train.
Underpinning the rise in overall income levels have been continued increases in entrepreneurial
earnings, which are up 12% over the past year. That will include stronger earnings in the agricultural
sector, which has been boosted by the strength in commodity export prices. That lift in agricultural
earnings has been reflected in stronger economic confidence and spending in many rural regions.
We expect commodity export earnings will remain firm over the year ahead. 
In contrast, wage and salary earnings are up just 2.7% over the past year. That’s up from 1.8% in the
year to March, but it’s a fairly muted rise, especially given the continued increases in the population
and inflation of 4.1%.
June’s large increase in savings builds on the solid increases seen over the past three years.
The savings rate in the June quarter was 3.7% of disposable incomes, down just slightly from last
quarter.
The value of households’ financial assets has continued to rise and is up 4.6% over the past year. 
However, the value of housing and land assets is down 0.9% over the past year, and it has remained
essentially unchanged since late 2023. We expect housing market conditions will remain soft over
the coming year, limiting increases in overall household wealth. 
Households’ spending on interest costs rose just slightly in the June quarter, climbing by 0.3%.
While that’s a relatively small change, it is the first increase that we’ve seen in two years.

### Page 3

Related articles

Media Contact:
Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist
P: +64 9 336 5668
M: +64 21 710 852
Over the past few years, many borrowers saw their debt servicing costs fall as they rolled off earlier
high mortgage rates and onto lower ones. That helped to support household spending.
However, the easing in average borrowing cost has now ended. Recent months have seen mortgage
rates pushing higher as the RBNZ has begun to hike the Official Cash Rate, with further hikes
expected over the coming year. 
Looking ahead, we don’t expect a significant increase in average household borrowing costs.
However, we won’t see the same support for spending that we did over the past few years.

## Document 343: IMF警告日本可能需要更快加息，全球债市再添压力？

Source PDF: all report pdf/IMF警告日本可能需要更快加息，全球债市再添压力？.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

IMF 罕⻅背书⽇本货币政策正常化，并提⽰若通胀超预期央⾏需加快加息。⽬前⽇本政策利
率已升⾄ 1.25% ，市场预计年内或再加息。随着⽇债收益率上升，⽇本资⾦加速撤离法债
等海外资产，加剧了全球债市的波动与承压。
摘要：
国际货币基⾦组织罕⻅地为⽇本货币政策正常化背书，并警告若通胀超预期，⽇本央⾏可能需
要加快加息步伐。这⼀表态，在全球债市已然动荡之际，令市场对⽇本资⾦撤离海外资产的担
忧进⼀步升温。
IMF 亚太部⻔主任 Krishna Srinivasan 在本周于曼⾕召开的 IMF 年会期间接受路透社采访时表⽰，
⽇本央⾏ " 正在做正确的事 " ，并强调若通胀上升快于预期， " 必须更快收紧，因为你不希望通胀
预期脱锚。这取决于数据。 "⽇本央⾏⾏⻓植⽥和男亦在出席本次 IMF 年会时重申，央⾏将专注
于将核⼼通胀锚定在 2% ⽬标附近，不会容忍任何超调，进⼀步强化了市场对年内再度加息的预
期。
IMF 的表态恰逢全球债市承压之际。法国 10 年期国债收益率⼀度逼近 5% ，创 2002 年以来新⾼，

借贷成本已⾼于希腊和意⼤利；美国 10 年期国债收益率攀升⾄ 5.28% 的数⼗年⾼位，即便通胀
数据⾛软也未能遏制抛售势头。在部分市场分析⼈⼠看来，⽇本政策正常化及由此引发的⽇本
资⾦回流，或是这两场动荡背后不可忽视的结构性⼒量。
⽇本央⾏正常化提速预期升温
⽇本央⾏已于今年 6 ⽉和 9 ⽉相继加息，将政策利率推升⾄ 1.25% ，为 31 年来最⾼。多数分析师
预计年内还将有⾄少⼀次加息。
Srinivasan 指出，⽇元疲软进⼀步推⾼了原材料进⼝成本，加剧了国内通胀压⼒，这也正是促
使⽇本央⾏持续收紧政策的重要背景之⼀。⽇本央⾏本⽉晚些时候将召开关键政策会议，市场
普遍预计届时利率将维持不变，但可能释放鹰派信号，为 12 ⽉加息铺路。
此外，中东冲突带来的通胀⻛险和⼈⼯智能需求旺盛，也在本次 IMF 年会上成为各国政策制定者
讨论的重要议题。
IMF ：⽇元⼲预不违背浮动汇率承诺
在汇率问题上， IMF 对⽇本当局的⽴场给予背书。 Srinivasan 表⽰，⽇本当局 " 完全致⼒于⾃由浮

动汇率制度 " ，他与⽇⽅进⾏了多次讨论，对此 " 毫不怀疑 " 。
IMF 在其亚洲经济报告的分析章节中指出， 2025 年年中以来⽇元的⼤幅波动，很⼤程度上与收
益率差等基本⾯因素关联不⼤，⽽是与⽇元期货的投机性仓位同步移动。⽇本于今年 7 ⽉ 31 ⽇与
美国联合进⾏了⼀次罕⻅的协调⼲预，以阻⽌⽇元跌⾄近 40 年低点。 Srinivasan 表⽰，⽇本汇市
深度充⾜，当局将汇率波动作为 " 第⼀道防线 " ，⼲预仅在特定情况下使⽤。
除货币政策外，⽇本的财政可持续性同样令市场存有疑虑。市场担⼼⽇本政府的⼤规模⽀出计
划，叠加明年 4 ⽉起为期两年的消费税下调，将进⼀步扩⼤本已庞⼤的债务规模。
Srinivasan 承认市场对财政⻛险的担忧，但也指出，疫情以来⽇本的财政整合⼒度实际上已超过
其他任何⼀个 G7 国家，部分原因在于税收收⼊的持续增⻓。他同时强调，⽇本需要明确新⽀出
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

⽇本资⾦撤离或加剧全球债市波动
全球债市的动荡，与⽇本政策正常化进程之间的关联正受到越来越多的关注。据彭博专栏作家
Gearoid Reidy 在分析⽂章中指出，世界或许并未为 " 有利率的⽇本 " 做好充分准备，⽽法国可能
只是第⼀个受到冲击的市场。
数据显⽰，截⾄今年 7 ⽉，⽇本投资者持有约 23 万亿⽇元（约合 1450 亿美元）法国国债，是⽇本
在欧元区最⼤的超配仓位。然⽽随着⽇本 10 年期国债收益率上⽉升破 3% ，创 30 年新⾼，经汇率
对冲后，法国 10 年期国债相对⽇本国债的收益率优势已收窄⾄约 40 个基点，吸引⼒⼤幅下滑。
收益率优势消退叠加法国财政状况恶化，⽇本资⾦已开始松动。数据显⽰，⽇本持有的法国国

债规模较去年底减少 2.5% 。三井住友 DS 资产管理旗下由 Shinji Kunibe 负责的全球固收基⾦已出
于财政担忧清仓法债。瑞穗的 Masayuki Nakajima 表⽰： " 即便估值看起来更便宜，⽇本投资者
重建仓位的动⼒也在下降。 "
随着 IMF 为⽇本加快加息提供政策⽀撑，⽇本国内资产吸引⼒的持续回升，或将使这⼀趋势延
续，并对依赖⽇本资⾦的海外债券市场形成持续的结构性压⼒。

## Document 345: Indonesia_ Policy mix aligns (2026-10-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Indonesia_ Policy mix aligns (2026-10-12).pdf
Report source: DBS
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

SHIFT IN NARRATIVE

After a rough first half of the year, the 
narrative around Indonesia’s economy has 
turned relatively constructive . The macro 
outlook is on the mend, and policy coherence 
has strengthened. Recent budget 
developments have reduced the likelihood of an 
outright fiscal slippage. A more disciplined 
fiscal trajectory should also provide room for 
monetary policy to stay focused on its price 
stability mandate rather than on ad dressing 
financial stability risks due to fiscal concerns.

On the markets front, e ven as external triggers 
impacted the rupiah, the one -sided sharp 
depreciation run in the currency in the first half 
of the year has stalled . Debt markets have  
attracted offshore interest, alongside strong 
demand for shorter-tenor SRBI securities, all of 
which have contributed to the overall capital 
flows picture.

Ahead of MSCI’s final take on the domestic 
equity market’s classification in November, 
however, equity markets  find little favour . 
Nonetheless, material improvement s in the 
stock listing s and regulatory requirements 
undertaken by the stock market and financial 
regulators are expected to contribute to the 
medium-term stability of the capital markets.

Key domestic risks that plagued the economy 
in the first half of the year have been alleviated 
to some extent, while a few other monitorables 
remain.

www.dbs.com/insightsdirect/macro groupresc@dbs.com 
• Indonesia's macro narrative has 
turned more constructive in 
2H26. 
• Key domestic policy concerns 
have eased. 
• Policy alignment has improved. 
• Greater fiscal discipline should 
allow BI to remain focused on 
price stability.  
• The current account is likely to 
widen as the trade surplus 
narrows. 
• Portfolio and FDI inflows should, 
however, narrow the BOP 
shortfall. 
• Few monitorables remain, 
including factors in the 2027 
fiscal math. 
• IDR performance will remain 
heavily influenced by global 
conditions.

INDONESIA: Policy mix aligns

12 OCTOBER 2026 
Radhika Rao, Senior Economist 
radhikarao@dbs.com

### Page 2

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            2

NEAR-TERM FISCAL WORRIES 
RECEDE

Fiscal fears have moderated in the near 
term. New Finance Minister Suahasil 
Nazara reaffirmed the government’s 
commitment to maintain the annual 
deficit below -3% of GDP threshold. The 
2026 run-rate suggests that the deficit is 
likely to close within the budgeted target.

By September 2026, the fiscal deficit 
stood at 1.2% of GDP , reflecting a 
normalisation in spending and firm 
revenue growth. Total spending rose by 
a still -strong 1 9% yoy , even though the 
pace moderated from 1H, backed by 
disbursements towards the flagship free 
food program, subsidy and 
compensation outlays , as well as 
personnel expenditure.  Realised 
expenditure stood at 69% of the full -
year budget by 9M26.

On the other hand, revenues rose sharply 
by 25 .5% yoy (partly benefiting from 
base effects as readings were negative 
at the same time last year), helped by 
higher tax collections, export duties, and 
non-tax receipts. Revenues have already 
accounted for 74% of the budgeted 
scale by 9M26, suggesting full -year 
target is likely to be met.

Global crude prices are a key risk for the 
fiscal outlook, as prevailing Brent prices 
are above $100bl at the time of writing 
(vs budgeted ICP at $70bl), which will 
feed through higher subsidy and 
compensation outgoes, as authorities 
remain hesitant to raise fuel pump prices.

Significant efficiency measures have, 
however, been put in place  to contain 
spending, including reductions in 
outlays towards flagship schemes , 
which will help contain the extent of 
deterioration in the fiscal math. For 
instance, the flagship MBG program, 
which started with an allocation of IDR 
35trn, was  moderated to IDR 268trn 
(realised spending by Sep26 was IDR 
153trn). Successive stimulus packages

### Page 3

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

have been announced but the scale has 
been contained.

2027 math signals fiscal prudence, soft 
spots warrant attention

The official goalposts for 2027 point to 
a continued preference for fiscal 
prudence rather than fiscal activism.

Spending is on course to surpass the 
symbolic IDR 4000tn level, ris ing nearly 
4% yoy, with outlays divided between 
the centre and regional governments. 
The mix points to steady allocations 
towards subsidies, social transfers , and 
capital expenditure,  with ministry -level 
spending (K/L) set to contract, while non-
K/L spending is budgeted to increase. 
More notably, spending is expected to 
rise on key flagship programs – for 
instance, social protection (up 10%), the 
largest component education (up 14%) , 
which includes the free meals program 
at IDR 240trn.

On the revenue end, state revenues are 
expected to rise by 9% yoy compared to 
the budgeted 2026 APBN/ 7% vs the 
revised 2026 outlook. Compared to 
baseline 2026, tax revenues are 
expected to rise 10.5%, besides higher 
non-tax collections. This math lands the 
2027 budgeted deficit at -2.4% of GDP 
vs revised 2026 at -2.85% of GDP.

Few uncertainties that loom.  Firstly, 
next year’s math rests on an upbeat 
growth projection of 6 .0% (DBSf 5.2%) , 
besides modest depreciation in the 
rupiah and about  a 7% increase in the 
Indonesia crude price assumption (see 
table). Some of these expectations 
might be challenged as the year unfolds. 
Next, state revenues are expected to 
rise to a n upbeat  12.5% of GDP , likely 
riding on a strong growth forecast, 
better tax due diligence , and improved 
administration via the Coretax 
mechanism.

Thirdly, crude prices are a key risk to the 
fiscal math. Given the economy’s 
position as a net oil importer, various 
official commentaries put the sensitivity  
at every $1pb oil worsening the fiscal 
balance by IDR 6trn, after accounting for 
oil-related revenues. This implies that for 
every $10pb increase, the deficit could 
be impacted by ~IDR 60trn, not far from 
MOF’s sensitivity of IDR 68trn (~0.25 -
0.30% of GDP) due to higher subsidy and 
compensation costs  (ICT assumed at 
$75bl vs prevailing Brent at $100bl). 
Spending will need to reprioritised to 
accommodate additional allocation 
towards subsidies and compensation.
